<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">placed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> PlacedSegment</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">[]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> boolean</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> } {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> valid</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> segments.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">filter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Number.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">isFinite</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(s.value) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> s.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> total</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> valid.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">reduce</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> s.value, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">total) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { placed: [], round: </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> spans</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> valid.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (s.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> circumference)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> single</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> valid.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> ===</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // A round cap reaches half the stroke width past each end of its dash (and</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // ACTIVE_GROW / 2 more on the hovered segment), so round-capped dashes are</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // trimmed by the stroke width plus a gap that is never smaller than that</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // extra reach. Round caps are only used when every segment is long enough to</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // absorb the trim; otherwise all segments use butt caps, whose ends stop</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">  // exactly at the pad, so neighbours can never overlap.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> capTrim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> thickness </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(gap, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">ACTIVE_GROW</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> round</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> !</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">single </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> spans.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">every</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> span </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> capTrim </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> thickness </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> trim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> single </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> :</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> round </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> capTrim </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> gap</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> cursor </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 0</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> placed</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> valid.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">map</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">((</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">i</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> span</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> spans[i]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> length</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> Math.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">max</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">, span </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> trim)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    const</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> seg</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657"> PlacedSegment</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">      ...</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">s,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      color: s.color </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">??</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> chartColor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(segments.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF">indexOf</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">(s)),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      share: s.value </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> total,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#8B949E">      // Centre whatever is left of a tiny segment inside its own span.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      start: cursor </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> (span </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> length) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">      length,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">    cursor </span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> span</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> seg</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">  return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> { placed, round }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72">function</span><span style="--shiki-light:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#D2A8FF"> DonutChart</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">({</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  gap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#79C0FF"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  formatValue</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3"> formatNumber,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  totalLabel</span><span style="--shiki-light:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#FF7B72"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#A5D6FF"> "Total"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  onActiveChange</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="--shiki-light:#953800;--shiki-dark:#FFA657">  className</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#E6EDF3">,</span></span>
